[PEASHOOTER_DESCRIPTION]
Peashooters are your first line of defense. They shoot peas at attacking zombies.
{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Damage:{STAT} normal
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}How can a single plant grow and shoot so many peas so quickly? Peashooter says,
"Hard work, commitment, and a healthy, well-balanced breakfast of sunlight and high-fiber
carbon dioxide make it all possible."

[SUNFLOWER_DESCRIPTION]
Sunflowers are essential for you to produce extra sun. Try planting as many as you can!
{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Sun production:{STAT} normal
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}Sunflower can't resist bouncing to the beat. Which beat is that? Why, the
life-giving jazzy rhythm of the Earth itself, thumping at a frequency only Sunflower can hear.

[WALL_NUT_DESCRIPTION]
Wall-nuts have hard shells which you can use to protect your other plants.
{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Toughness:{STAT} high
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}"People wonder how I feel about getting constantly chewed on by zombies," says Wall-nut.
"What they don't realize is that with my limited senses all I can feel is a kind of tingling, like a relaxing back rub."

[CHERRY_BOMB_DESCRIPTION]
Cherry Bombs can blow up all zombies in an area. They have a short fuse so plant them near zombies.
{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Damage:{STAT} massive
{KEYWORD}Range:{STAT} all zombies in a medium area
{KEYWORD}Usage:{STAT} single use, instant
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}"I wanna explode," says Cherry #1. "No, let's detonate instead!" says
his brother, Cherry #2. After intense consultation they agree to explodonate.

[POTATO_MINE_DESCRIPTION]
Potato Mines pack a powerful punch, but they need a while to arm themselves. You should plant them ahead of zombies. They will explode on contact.
{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Damage:{STAT} massive
{KEYWORD}Range:{STAT} all zombies in a small area
{KEYWORD}Usage:{STAT} single use, delayed activation
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}Some folks say Potato Mine is lazy, that he leaves everything to the last
minute. Potato Mine says nothing. He's too busy thinking about his investment strategy.

[REPEATER_DESCRIPTION]
Repeaters fire two peas at a time.
{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Damage:{STAT} normal (for each pea)
{KEYWORD}Firing speed:{STAT} 2x
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}Repeater is fierce. He's from the streets. He doesn't take attitude from anybody,
plant or zombie, and he shoots peas to keep people at a distance. Secretly, though, Repeater yearns for love.

[SNOW_PEA_DESCRIPTION]
Snow Peas shoot frozen peas that damage and slow the enemy.
{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Damage:{STAT} normal, slows zombies
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}Folks often tell Snow Pea how "cool" he is, or exhort him to "chill out." They
tell him to "stay frosty." Snow Pea just rolls his eyes. He's heard 'em all.

[CHOMPER_DESCRIPTION]
Chompers can devour a zombie whole, but they are vulnerable while chewing.

{KEYWORD}Damage:{STAT} massive
{KEYWORD}Range:{STAT} very short
{KEYWORD}Special:{STAT} long delay between chomps
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}Chomper almost got a gig doing stunts in Hollywood but it fell
through when his agent demanded too much on the front end. Chomper's not resentful,
though. He says it's just part of the business.

{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Damage:{STAT} normal

{NOCTURNAL}Sleeps during the day
{SHORTLINE}

[SUN_SHROOM_DESCRIPTION]

{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Sun production:{STAT} low, then normal
{NOCTURNAL}Sleeps during the day
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}Sun-shroom hates sun. He hates it so much that when it builds up in his system,

{SHORTLINE}

This zombie shoots peas at your plants.
{SHORTLINE}
{KEYWORD}Toughness:{STAT} low {270}
{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}A rare sort, Peashooter Zombie has a Peashooter for a head and fires peas. This is why our scientists have called him Peashooter Zombie. They're very clever people you know.
Now, you might enjoy peas with your dinner, but when you combine a pea and a zombie you're in for big trouble. Big green trouble. That fires peas. Dinner doesn't sound so appealing now, eh?!

{SHORTLINE}
{FLAVOR}Wall-nut Zombie has a Wall-nut for a head. He's a tough nut to crack. Get it? A nut?
Being half nut, Wall-nut Zombie knows how to crack a tough shell and reach the nut inside. By shell, he means your skull. By nut, he means your brain. Sorry about that!

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_GAMEPADTRIGGERS]
Choose what LT/RT (ZL/ZR on Switch) do in battle. Use items (default): select a top-bar item, then press Confirm to use it. Auto-collect: press either trigger to collect resources such as sun and coins across the board. Holding a trigger does not repeat. You can still press Up on the D-pad to reach items. Menu, seed selection and garden paging, and the automatic sun/coin collection settings are unchanged.

[NEW_OPTIONS_ENABLE_ENDLESS_STAR]
Enable Flying Imp

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_ENABLEENDLESSSTAR]
When off, the Flying Imp and the star on the lawn will not appear in applicable Adventure and Journey to the West levels. These levels can award at most two stars.
Changing this setting does not affect a battle in progress. It takes effect when you restart the level. Continuing a saved game keeps its original state. Previous star records are kept.

[NEW_OPTIONS_SCREEN_STRETCH]
Screen stretch

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SCREENSTRETCH]
0% keeps the original screen aspect ratio. 100% uses a 4:3 layout stretched to fill the screen. Intermediate values blend between them. This also applies to tall screens, with the same 4:3 endpoint. Stretching changes shapes and the visible area. Drag and release to apply; keyboard or controller changes apply immediately. Saved only on this device.

[NEW_OPTIONS_MOUSE_CURSOR_SIZE]
Cursor size

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_MOUSECURSORSIZE]
Set the game cursor size to 0, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2, 2.5 or 3 (default: 1). At 0, use the system cursor: the game only requests the normal, link or text type, while the system controls its appearance and size. Changes apply immediately.

[NEW_OPTIONS_OPEN_SOURCE_LICENSES]
Open-source Licenses

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_LANGUAGE]
Choose Chinese, English or Spanish for the game interface.
Restart the game to apply a language change. Changing the language does not erase player progress or change level content.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_MUSICVOLUME]
Adjust background music from 0% to 100%. Drag the slider to hear the change.
Setting it to 0% turns music off; raising it enables music again. Plant, zombie and button sounds are controlled separately by SFX Volume.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SOUNDVOLUME]
Adjust plant, zombie, button and other sound effects from 0% to 100%.
Set it to 0% to mute sound effects. Background music is controlled separately by Music Volume.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_FULLSCREEN]
Switch between windowed and full-screen display. Full screen lets the game use the whole display area.
This option is available only on platforms that allow full-screen switching. Devices locked to full screen do not show it.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_RUNLOCKED]
Allow the game to keep running when its window loses focus or is minimized. This can be useful when waiting for a level to progress.
When off, the normal focus-loss pause rules apply. Background play still uses system resources, and a mobile operating system may suspend the app.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_VIBRATION]
Allow game events to trigger vibration on the device or current controller. Both use this switch.
Feedback requires compatible hardware. Turning this on cannot add vibration support to a controller that lacks it. Turning it off stops current controller vibration.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_LOWLATENCYAUDIO]
Enable low-latency audio mode to shorten the output buffer and reduce the delay between an action and its sound feedback.
Restart the game after changing it; some devices or drivers may not support this mode.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_CAMERASIZE]
Adjust the battlefield zoom. Drag left to make plants and zombies appear larger, or right to see more of the scene. Seed banks and menus stay the same size.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_TOPSEEDBANK]
Place the seed bank on the left or at the top of the screen. This changes its layout, not the number of cards you can carry.
On narrow screens, the left layout may cover part of the battlefield or controls. The game asks for confirmation when needed.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_BOARDLEFTOFFSET]
Adjust the position of left-side interface elements within the extra space on wide screens. Relevant main-menu and battlefield layouts use this setting.
Move the slider to suit your screen edges and reach. The effect may be small when there is no extra horizontal space.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_BOARDRIGHTOFFSET]
Adjust right-side interface elements within extra wide-screen space, independently of the left margin.
Use it to position right-side controls more comfortably. The available movement depends on the screen aspect ratio and current layout.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_COVERLAYER]
Choose the foreground cover style: none, grass or objects.
Pick the appearance and visibility you prefer. Changing it also adjusts relevant street-zombie positions during seed selection to match the selected cover.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_DRAWCROSSHAIR]
Show a crosshair when choosing where to plant, making the target row and column easier to identify, especially with touch or a controller.
It is a visual guide: it does not plant automatically or change placement rules.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SHOWGAMEPADHINTS]
Show available actions for gamepads and keyboards in Gamepad mode, using actual key names for the keyboard. Local two-player hints stay visible for devices assigned to P1 and P2: the mouse adds no icon and each gamepad shows its own icons. Shared actions use one label.
Changes apply and save immediately. Hiding hints does not affect controls, focus outlines or Keyboard mode help.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SHOWTOOLTIPS]
Allow contextual tooltips for supported cards, objects and controls. They provide information about the item you point at.
Availability depends on the screen, target and input method. Turn this off to reduce tooltip panels on screen.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_MAINMENUZOMBIEHAND]
Play the zombie-hand opening animation when starting Adventure from the main menu.
Turning it off skips the usual animation to reach the next screen sooner. Certain flows, such as the first game start, may still play it.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_ZOMBIEAPPEARANCE]
Choose big-head or small-head zombie proportions.
This is an appearance setting; it does not change zombie health, attack power or level difficulty.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_DANCERAPPEARANCE]
Switch between the MJ and Disco styles for the corresponding dancing and backup-dancer zombies.
The styles differ in behavior as well as appearance, including movement distance, so this choice can affect how a level plays out.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SPECIALSHADERS]
Enable animated pool and flame effects and soft-edged, multi-pointer vision.
When disabled or unsupported, pool effects are hidden, flames use a fixed image, and limited view uses one hard-edged circle.
Changes take effect immediately. Built-in color filters are unaffected.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_ENHANCEDISPLAY]
Show extra card-cooldown and plant/zombie health information to help you judge the battle and time your actions.
This adds more numbers and information to the screen. The exact information depends on the mode and object type.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_GRAPHICSBACKEND]
Choose a graphics rendering backend supported by this platform. Auto is a good starting point; another backend may help with graphics or compatibility problems.
Restart the game after changing it. Compatibility and performance depend on your device and drivers.

[NEW_OPTIONS_APPLY_SPEED_ON_NEW_LEVEL]
Start with speed boost

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_APPLYSPEEDONNEWLEVEL]
When enabled, new levels immediately use your configured speed. Off by default: new levels start at 1x, and the in-game speed button enables your configured speed.
This setting does not change the speed state of the current level.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SPEED]
Adjust gameplay speed from 1x to 10x. 1x is normal speed. Higher values reduce waiting time but also give you less time to react.
Specific modes or multiplayer rules may restrict the speed actually used.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_AUTOCOLLECTSUN]
Automatically collect available sun to reduce repeated clicks. When off, collect it manually as usual.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_AUTOCOLLECTCOIN]
Automatically collect available coins to reduce repeated clicks. When off, collect them manually.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_DRAGPLANTING]
Allow dragging a card to its target position and releasing it to plant.
When off, use the select-card-then-click-position interaction. Planting still requires enough sun, a ready card and a valid location.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_ONLYORIGINALFEATURES]
Use the original-content menu and related entry points, hiding or replacing the corresponding expanded-content entries.
Restart the game after changing it. Existing progress is not deleted; turn it off and restart to access the expanded entries again.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SHOWTOOLS]
Show buttons for power-ups such as +500 Sun, Cherry Windstorm and clearing cooldowns.
Showing the buttons does not make power-ups free; their normal costs still apply. Permanently Free Power-ups is a separate feature.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SHOWUNLOCKBUTTON]
Show the main-menu unlock button so you can open the relevant unlock flow.
This only controls the entry point. It does not directly unlock levels or other content; the normal unlock flow still applies.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_ENABLESL]
Show S and L buttons in the in-game settings. S saves the current level; L loads the saved level for quick saving and loading.
Loading returns to the saved state and discards unsaved level progress. Availability still depends on the current mode and multiplayer restrictions.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_FREEGOLDFINGER]
Enable free power-ups permanently for the current player, removing their gold-ingot costs. A confirmation flow appears before activation.
This is permanent and cannot be turned off through settings after activation. Power-up button visibility is controlled separately.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_IMPORTINTERNALDATA]
Select a save ZIP exported by this game. After validation, it is imported on the next launch. Export a backup first.
Only player profiles, game progress, custom levels and Zombatar images are replaced.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_EXPORTINTERNALDATA]
Package current save data into a game-specific ZIP for backup or transfer. Export attempts to save the current level and player data first.
Only player profiles, game progress, custom levels and Zombatar images are included. Restore this ZIP through Import Save.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_OPENINTERNALFOLDER]
Open the internal game-data directory in the system file manager to inspect or manually back up files.
Editing, moving or deleting files there can affect saves and custom content, so keep a backup before doing so. This entry appears only on supported platforms.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_USERDATAFORMAT]
Choose the format used to save player data: MessagePack is compact binary data; JSON is readable, editable text.
Keeping the default MessagePack is suitable for normal play. Changing this does not immediately batch-convert every existing file or replace backup import/export.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_LEVELDATAFORMAT]
Choose the format used to save a running level: compact binary MessagePack or readable, editable JSON.
This concerns saved gameplay states, not importing custom-level definitions. Normal play does not require changing the default. Back up files before editing them manually.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_CHRISTMAS]
Enable Christmas decorations: Santa hats on some zombies and stockings in some scenes.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_MUSTACHE]
Give supported zombies mustaches as a cosmetic Easter egg, without changing their combat abilities. You can also type mustache in the game to toggle it.
This is not saved as a persistent setting.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_TRICKEDOUT]
Use the hot-rod appearance for supported lawn mowers. This is a cosmetic Easter egg, also available by typing trickedout.
This is not saved as a persistent setting.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_FUTURE]
Give supported zombies futuristic glasses without changing their combat abilities. You can also type future to toggle it.
This is not saved as a persistent setting.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_DAISIES]
Leave flowers when eligible zombies die. This is a visual Easter egg, not a controllable plant. You can also type daisies to toggle it.
Requires a Tree of Wisdom at least 100 feet tall; some zombies or terrain do not produce flowers. This is not saved as a persistent setting.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_DANCE]
Make supported zombies dance as they walk. You can also type dance to toggle it.
Requires a Tree of Wisdom at least 500 feet tall. Not every zombie type or action supports it. This is not saved as a persistent setting.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_PINATA]
Produce a burst of candy when eligible zombies die. The candy is a visual effect, not extra coins or collectible rewards. You can also type pinata to toggle it.
Requires a Tree of Wisdom at least 1000 feet tall. This is not saved as a persistent setting.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_SUKHBIR]
Replace the usual groans of some zombies with an alternate set of sounds. You can also type sukhbir to toggle it.
It does not change combat abilities. This is not saved as a persistent setting.

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_RESOURCEPACKLUASCRIPTS]
Allow Lua scripts supplied by resource packs to run. Scripts can extend the interface and gameplay, and may access files, make network requests or modify saves. Enable scripts only from sources you trust.
This is separate from enabling a resource pack itself. Restart as prompted after changing resource-pack settings. In multiplayer, check that both sides use compatible script configurations.

[NEW_OPTIONS_SWITCH_OFF]

[MAIL_RELEASE_TITLE]
{VERSION} Update notes

[MAIL_NOTE_1]
Zombie letter · Day

[MAIL_NOTE_2]
Zombie letter · Night

[MAIL_NOTE_3]
Zombie letter · Pool

[MAIL_NOTE_4]
Zombie letter · Fog

[MAIL_NOTE_5]
Dr. Zomboss's challenge

[MAIL_NOTE_CREDITS]
A letter after victory

[MAIL_RELEASE_VERSION]
2.4.3

[MAIL_RELEASE_BODY]
October 10, 2026

New
1. Oriental Bamboo and Beibei Grass are now available in the garden.

Balance
1. Squash can damage Little Spirit zombies, but still cannot target them.
2. Added lawn mowers to Vasebreaker.
3. Level 1-7 now locks the selection to six seed packets, matching the mushroom selection in the Endless Edition.
4. Plant deaths by crushing and similar effects now count toward secret-level conditions.

Improvements
1. Updated the Qin Emperor Zombie and Japanese Shogun animations.
2. Improved the font used for the Endless round counter.
3. Added mouse pointer size settings.
4. Added more controller layouts and live help; LB and RB now use tools.
5. The Almanac now lists plant drop levels. Tieba plants and zombies are ordered by appearance.
6. Gourd Kid immediately pauses a zombie's sound when absorbing it.
7. Little Spirit zombies can now be disabled.
8. Reduced save overhead for hero actions.

Fixes
1. Corrected the Slot Machine 3 level name.
2. Fixed Bajie Squash summoning multiple rakes.
3. Garden tool quantities can no longer fall below zero.
4. Fixed a crash when changing level-selection pages with a controller.
5. Fixed controller focus being lost when switching gardens.
6. Fixed the Journey to the West boss hint appearing when carrying Ice Jalapeno without Ice-shroom.

Platforms
1. Changed Windows controller input to resolve stuttering on some devices.
2. Deferred edge system gestures on iOS.

Custom levels
1. End-of-level zombies emerging from graves, pools or the sky can now be disabled.

Resource packs
1. Fixed the appearance of mod-added plants in seed selection.

[A11Y_ENABLE]
Accessibility narration

[A11Y_VOLUME]
Narration volume

[A11Y_RATE]
Narration speed

[A11Y_ENABLED]
Accessibility narration enabled. F9 reads the controls; F10 stops speech.

[A11Y_HELP]
Turning narration on selects Gamepad keyboard mode; turning it off selects Shortcuts. You can then change Controls > Keyboard mode separately. Only that choice determines keyboard controls. Volume and rate can also be adjusted while narration is off.

Menus: arrows, Tab, Enter, Esc.
In menus, F2 reads the current control details and F4 reads the page content.

Solo controls in Gamepad mode: arrows move by cell, J confirms or plants, K cancels, Q/E change cards. With empty hands, hold K for over one second, then release to dig. U performs the level action, Z/C use the configured trigger action, and Enter pauses.
Queries: F1 repeat, F2 current cell, F3 sun and coins, F4 wave, F5 zombies in this row, F6 current card. F9 help, F10 stop narration.
Multiplayer keeps existing controls. Narration requires an installed voice for your language and can be disabled under Controls.

[NEW_OPTIONS_CATEGORY_CONTROLS]
Controls

[NEW_OPTIONS_KEYBOARD_MODE]
Keyboard mode

[NEW_OPTIONS_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUTS]
Shortcuts

[NEW_OPTIONS_KEYBOARD_GAMEPAD]
Gamepad

[NEW_OPTIONS_HELP_KEYBOARDMODE]
Choose Shortcuts (default) or Gamepad for solo battles. Only this choice determines keyboard controls. Toggling narration selects Gamepad when enabled and Shortcuts when disabled; you can then change the mode here separately. Gamepad mode uses fixed keys:
WASD: left stick (move); arrows: D-pad
J / K / U / I: A / B / X / Y
Q / E: LB / RB (cards); Z / C: LT / RT
Numpad 8 / 2 / 4 / 6: right stick
Enter: Start; Backspace: Back; Esc: existing menu
In standard battles, J plants and K cancels. With empty hands, hold K over a plant for more than one second, then release to dig it up. U performs the level action.
Local two-player mode follows lobby device assignments; online play keeps existing shortcuts. Mapping pauses in text fields, dialogs and while paused. Release all mapped keys before resuming.
